- Talking: 3 individual bounce keyframes with 120ms stagger (will-talk-1/2/3)

- Listening: synchronous scale pulse 1.0→1.15→1.0 with opacity variation (1.5s)
- Streaming: sequential wave pulse per dot with 200ms stagger (typing indicator effect)
- Success: one-shot jump + bounce landing (~600ms), reverts to idle via useEffect timeout
- Error: one-shot horizontal shake (±2px, ~300ms), reverts to idle
- All states use CSS transition: all 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1) for smooth state changes
- Test page at /test-will-dots with interactive state switcher

## Session 2026-03-07 - Features #501, #507, #505

### Feature #501: sanitizeForAI utility functie voor data minimalisatie - PASSING
- Created lib/ai/sanitize.ts with sanitizeForAI(), sanitizeTransaction(), birthDateToAge() exports
- Regex patterns: IBAN (NL + international), BSN (9-digit), email, phone, street address, postal code
- Name replacement: sorted longest-first, individual parts >=3 chars, Dutch prepositions excluded
- Birth date: ISO + DD-MM-YYYY formats → age in years
- Transaction sanitisation: strips description + counterparty_name, keeps category + amount + month
- Financial amounts (€), percentages (%), ratios preserved (not filtered)

- Verified complete implementation of household cost splitting with 4 calculation modes
- All 11/11 verification tests passing at /api/verify-cost-splitting
- All 7 feature steps verified:
  1. **50/50 equal split**: computeSharePct returns 50% for 'equal' mode, calculateCostSplit computes both partner shares
  2. **Income-ratio proportional split**: Calculates myIncome/(myIncome+partnerIncome)*100, rounds to integer, falls back to 50% when total income is 0
  3. **Custom percentage split with user input**: Uses customSplitPct (default 50) with primaryPayerId, UI range slider (0-100) with clamping and real-time updates
  4. **One-pays-all mode**: Returns 100% for primary payer and 0% for partner via primaryPayerId
  5. **Apply splitting to shared budgets**: SharedBudgetSplitList filters shared budgets, calculates per-partner amounts, shows individual and total breakdowns
  6. **Apply splitting to shared debts**: SharedDebtSplitList splits both balance AND monthly payment per partner with 'Saldo' and 'Aflossing' display
  7. **Per-partner contribution UI**: PartnerContributionSummary, ContributionBar, CostSplitCalculator (mode selector + compare all modes + custom slider), compact and full modes

## Session 2026-02-18 - Feature #255

### Feature #255: Smart next step algorithm prioritization - PASSING
- Verified complete implementation of smart next step algorithm prioritization
- All 12/12 verification tests passing at /api/verify-next-step-prioritization
- All 8 feature steps verified:
  1. **Priority ordering implemented**: computeAllKernSteps and API both enforce P1-P5 ordering
  2. **Highest priority: no transactions imported**: import_transactions at position 0 in priority chain
  3. **Second priority: no assets added**: add_assets follows import_transactions
  4. **Third priority: budget categories over limit**: budget_attention follows add_assets, uses alertBudgetCount
  5. **Fourth priority: no goals set**: set_goals follows budget_attention, checks hasGoals
  6. **Fifth priority: FIRE target unreachable**: fire_unreachable follows set_goals, links to /horizon
  7. **Limit display to 1-2 suggestions per page**: NextStepSection renders only first non-dismissed step
  8. **Priority changes as user completes steps**: Dynamic conditions based on real data state

#### Implementation Architecture:
- **Client-side (computeAllKernSteps)**: Conditionally pushes steps based on data flags (hasTransactions, totalAssets, alertBudgetCount, hasGoals, fireUnreachable)
- **Server-side (/api/next-steps)**: Parallel Supabase queries for all data states, builds step list with priority numbers 1-9
- **Core page**: Fetches hasGoals and fireUnreachable from live data, passes to computeAllKernSteps
- **FIRE reachability**: Calculated via compound interest projection (7% annual return, 2% inflation, 50-year horizon, 4% SWR)
- **All 3 module pages**: Core uses computeAllKernSteps, Will uses computeAllWilSteps, Horizon uses computeAllHorizonSteps

#### Key Files (all previously built by other agents):
- **components/app/next-step-card.tsx**: computeAllKernSteps, computeAllWilSteps, computeAllHorizonSteps, NextStepSection, NextStepCard
- **app/api/next-steps/route.ts**: GET endpoint with parallel data queries, priority-ordered step list, FIRE calculation
- **app/api/next-steps/complete/route.ts**: POST with fire_unreachable in VALID_STEP_KEYS
- **app/(app)/core/page.tsx**: Passes hasGoals and fireUnreachable to computeAllKernSteps
- **app/api/verify-next-step-prioritization/route.ts**: 12-test verification endpoint
- **app/test-next-step-prioritization/page.tsx**: Visual test page with 6 priority scenarios

#### Verification:
- 12/12 API verification tests passing
- Browser verified: test page shows ALL TESTS PASSING with green checkmarks
- All 6 priority scenarios render correct first step
- Max 1-2 rule enforced: queue has 6 steps but only 1 card shown
- API returns 401 for unauthenticated access

- Console errors: only expected 401s from unauthenticated NextStepSection API calls (graceful fallback)

- Verified the freedom card generator never exposes sensitive EUR amounts by default

## Session 2026-02-18 - Feature #251

### Feature #251: Spending variance confidence indicator - PASSING
- Verified complete implementation of spending variance confidence indicators
- All 27/27 verification tests passing at /api/verify-spending-variance-confidence
- All 5 feature steps verified:
  1. **Calculate standard deviation of spending per budget category**: calculateSpendingVariance() computes mean, variance, stdDev from non-zero monthly amounts. /api/budget-variance maps over all parent budgets with child transaction aggregation
  2. **Determine confidence level: low variance = high confidence**: Coefficient of Variation (CV = stdDev/mean) mapping: CV < 0.15 = high (90%), CV 0.15-0.35 = medium (75%), CV > 0.35 = low (45%). Same logic in both spending-confidence-indicator.tsx and budget-forecast.ts
  3. **Create visual confidence indicator component**: Three components — SpendingConfidenceIndicator (1-3 WiFi-style signal bars), SpendingConfidenceBadge (compact pill with bars + Dutch label), SpendingVarianceDetailPanel (full breakdown with stats grid + progress bar). Color-coded: emerald=high, amber=medium, zinc=low
  4. **Display on budget forecast predictions**: Budget page imports and renders SpendingConfidenceBadge in forecast card header and SpendingVarianceDetailPanel below. calculateSpendingVariance called with monthly spending history
  5. **Handle categories with insufficient data**: < 3 months returns confidence="insufficient" with 0 bars, Dutch message "Onvoldoende data", separate rendering path in forecast section

#### Key Files (all previously built by other agents):
- **components/app/spending-confidence-indicator.tsx**: calculateSpendingVariance, SpendingConfidenceIndicator, SpendingConfidenceBadge, SpendingVarianceDetailPanel

## Session 2026-02-18 - Feature #243

### Feature #243: Household FIRE projections - PASSING
- Verified complete implementation of household FIRE projections with partner comparison
- All 6 feature steps verified and implemented:
  1. **Calculate combined household income from both partners**: API fetches transactions for all household members, sums positive amounts as monthlyIncome per member, combines into combinedMonthlyIncome
  2. **Calculate combined household expenses**: API fetches negative transactions, computes monthlyExpenses per member, handles personal vs shared asset/debt ownership split
  3. **Compute shared FIRE target based on combined data**: Uses computeFireProjection() with combined HorizonInput (all personal + shared assets/debts counted once). FIRE target = yearlyExpenses / 0.04 (25x rule)
  4. **Compute individual FIRE targets per partner**: Each partner gets personal assets + 50% of shared assets, personal debts + 50% of shared debts. Individual computeFireProjection() and computeFireRange() calls
  5. **Create partner comparison view**: HouseholdFireSection (544 lines) with combined hero card, 5 financial summary cards, 2 PartnerCards, 5 ComparisonBars (net worth, income, savings rate, freedom %, FIRE age), "Samen sterker" insight
  6. **Integrate with existing FIRE projection logic**: Uses computeFireProjection/computeFireRange from lib/horizon-data.ts, formatCurrency/formatFireAge, HouseholdFireSection rendered on /horizon page

#### Key Files:
- **components/app/household-fire-section.tsx**: Full-featured UI component (544 lines) with combined card, partner cards, comparison bars, insight section
- **app/api/household/fire-projections/route.ts**: GET endpoint (391 lines) fetching all financial data for household members in parallel
- **lib/household-data.ts**: Pure utility functions for split mode calculations, perspective filtering, net worth computation

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #228

### Feature #228: Transaction log per holding - PASSING
- Implemented complete transaction log per holding with running P&L calculations
- All 7 feature steps verified and implemented:
  1. **Transaction log UI per holding**: HoldingTransactionLog component with expandable rows showing transaction details and running P&L stats
  2. **Buy transaction**: Adds units, updates weighted average cost basis (currentUnits * currentAvg + newUnits * newPrice) / totalUnits
  3. **Sell transaction**: Reduces units, calculates realized P&L = (sell_price - avg_cost) * units_sold
  4. **Dividend event**: Records income in cumulative_dividends without changing units
  5. **Running P&L per holding**: Summary cards showing total invested, realized P&L, unrealized P&L, dividend income, total return
  6. **Transaction history chronologically**: Computed chronologically for correct P&L, displayed newest first
  7. **API: GET/POST /api/holdings/[id]/transactions**: RESTful route with P&L annotations, dual-source support (holding_transactions table + valuations fallback)

#### New Files:
- **app/api/holdings/[id]/transactions/route.ts**: RESTful API with computeRunningPnL() function annotating each transaction with running_units, running_cost_basis, running_avg_price, realized_pnl, cumulative_realized_pnl, cumulative_dividends
- **components/app/holding-transaction-log.tsx**: HoldingTransactionLog component with:
  - P&L summary cards (5-card grid)
  - Expandable transaction rows with running stats
  - Inline transaction form with buy/sell/dividend type selector

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #211

### Feature #211: Automatic monthly snapshots - PASSING
- Implemented dual-mechanism auto-snapshot system:
  1. Client-side: AutoSnapshotTrigger component in app layout fires GET /api/snapshots/auto on every authenticated page load
  2. Server-side: GET /api/snapshots/cron for external cron services (Vercel Cron, pg_cron, etc.)
- Auto-captures all 8 metrics per snapshot:
  - net_worth (total_assets - total_debts)
  - total_assets (sum of active assets)
  - total_debts (sum of active debts)
  - freedom_percentage ((net_worth / FIRE_target) * 100)
  - fire_age (via computeFireProjection; null if no DOB)
  - sovereignty_level (-2 to 6 via computeSovereigntyLevel)
  - savings_rate (via fire projection)
  - resilience_score (0-100 via computeResilienceScore)
- Idempotent: only one snapshot per month per user (checks monthStart before creating)
- No 24-record cap — unlimited history kept
- Cron endpoint (/api/snapshots/cron) processes ALL onboarded users via service_role key
- Protected by CRON_SECRET env var; graceful fallback when not configured
- 9/9 verification tests passing at /api/verify-auto-snapshots
- Zero console errors
- No mock data patterns found

### Changes Made
- lib/hooks/use-auto-snapshot.ts: Created hook for fire-and-forget auto-snapshot on mount
- components/app/auto-snapshot-trigger.tsx: Client component wrapper for the hook
- app/(app)/layout.tsx: Added AutoSnapshotTrigger to app layout
- app/api/snapshots/cron/route.ts: Created server-side cron endpoint for all-user snapshot creation
- app/api/verify-auto-snapshots/route.ts: Created 9-test verification endpoint
- app/test-auto-snapshots/page.tsx: Created visual test page with live API test
- lib/supabase/proxy.ts: Added test routes to public paths

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #204

### Feature #204: Badge evaluation engine - PASSING
- Built background badge evaluation engine with triggers after key actions
- Enhanced POST /api/badges/evaluate to accept optional `trigger` parameter
  - Valid triggers: login, action_complete, import, month_close, page_load, manual
  - Trigger included in response for tracking/debugging

#### Trigger Integration Points:
1. **Login**: app/login/page.tsx — calls /api/badges/evaluate with trigger: 'login' after successful signInWithPassword (fire-and-forget)
2. **Action completion**: components/app/action-board.tsx — imports useBadgeEvaluation hook, calls evaluateBadges('action_complete') when handleStatusChange sets status to 'completed'
3. **Bank file import**: app/(app)/core/cash/import/page.tsx — calls /api/badges/evaluate with trigger: 'import' after successful import (setStep(4))
4. **Month close/snapshot**: app/api/snapshots/auto/route.ts and app/api/snapshots/route.ts — server-side fetch to /api/badges/evaluate with trigger: 'month_close' after snapshot creation
5. **Page load**: components/app/badge-evaluator.tsx — passes 'page_load' trigger (already on dashboard and identity pages)

#### Key Design Decisions:
- All triggers are fire-and-forget (.catch(() => {})) — badges are non-critical, should never block main flow
- Login and import triggers use direct fetch() calls (no hook dependency needed)
- Action completion uses the useBadgeEvaluation hook for toast notifications
- Snapshot triggers use server-side fetch with cookie forwarding for auth context
- useBadgeEvaluation hook updated to accept trigger parameter (typed as BadgeEvaluationTrigger)
- Existing idempotency preserved: Set<string> of existing slugs, 11 existingEarned.has() checks

#### Verification:
- 12/12 tests passing at /api/verify-badge-evaluation-engine
- Zero console errors
- No mock data patterns found
- Browser verified with playwright

### Changes Made
- app/api/badges/evaluate/route.ts: Added VALID_TRIGGERS, trigger parsing from request body, trigger in all responses
- lib/hooks/use-badge-evaluation.ts: Added BadgeEvaluationTrigger type, trigger parameter to evaluateBadges()
- components/app/badge-evaluator.tsx: Pass 'page_load' trigger
- app/login/page.tsx: Fire-and-forget badge evaluation after login
- components/app/action-board.tsx: Import useBadgeEvaluation, trigger on action completion
- app/(app)/core/cash/import/page.tsx: Fire-and-forget badge evaluation after import
- app/api/snapshots/auto/route.ts: Server-side badge evaluation after auto-snapshot
- app/api/snapshots/route.ts: Server-side badge evaluation after manual snapshot

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #178

### Feature #178: formatWithFreedom() utility function - PASSING
- Created formatWithFreedom() utility in lib/format.ts
- Core philosophy utility: "Geld is opgeslagen tijd" — converts EUR amounts to freedom-time equivalents
- Three exported functions:
  1. calculateFreedomTime(amount, dailyExpenses) → { years, months, days, totalDays }
  2. formatFreedomTimeString(breakdown, format, includeDays) → Dutch formatted string
  3. formatWithFreedom(amount, dailyExpenses, options) → "€ 450.000 (12 jaar en 4 maanden)"
- Supports long format: "12 jaar en 4 maanden"
- Supports short format: "12j 4m"
- Edge cases handled:
  - 0 daily expenses → "∞ vrijheid"
  - Negative amounts → shows absolute freedom time equivalent
  - Very large amounts → capped at 9999 years
  - Zero amount → "0 dagen"
  - Singular "maand" vs plural "maanden"
- 12/12 verification tests passing at /api/verify-format-with-freedom
- Zero console errors
- No mock data patterns found (grep clean)
- No database persistence needed (pure utility function)

### Changes Made
- lib/format.ts: Added formatWithFreedom(), calculateFreedomTime(), formatFreedomTimeString(), FreedomTimeBreakdown and FormatWithFreedomOptions interfaces
- app/api/verify-format-with-freedom/route.ts: Created 12-test verification endpoint

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #177

### Feature #177: FIRE scenario defaults to current financial parameters - PASSING
- Horizon page at app/(app)/horizon/page.tsx loads all FIRE parameters from real Supabase data
- Data flow: 9 parallel Supabase queries → HorizonInput object → computeFireProjection() → UI display
- Sources:
  - monthlyIncome: transactions table (positive amounts this month)
  - monthlyExpenses: transactions table (absolute negative amounts this month)
  - totalAssets: assets table (sum of current_value where is_active=true)
  - totalDebts: debts table (sum of current_balance where is_active=true)
  - yearlyMustExpenses: budgets table (essential expenses, interval-adjusted)
  - savingsRate: computed as (income - expenses) / income * 100
  - dateOfBirth: profiles table
- Added Spaarquote (savings rate) card to FIRE inputs section (data-testid="input-savings-rate")
- Added Jaarlijkse vaste lasten (annual expenses from budgets) card (data-testid="input-annual-expenses")
- Grid changed from lg:grid-cols-4 to lg:grid-cols-3 to accommodate 6 cards

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #176

### Feature #176: Collapsible sections default to collapsed state - PASSING
- CollapsibleSection component at components/app/collapsible-section.tsx
- Component prop: `defaultOpen` defaults to `false` — sections start collapsed
- State initialization: `useState(defaultOpen)` sets isOpen to false by default
- Summary preview: `{!isOpen && summary && <p>...` shows 1-line preview when collapsed

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #165

### Feature #165: Deleting budget category updates spending totals - PASSING
- Created DELETE /api/budgets/[id] API endpoint with full cascade cleanup:
  1. Validates UUID format (returns 400 for invalid IDs)
  2. Requires authentication (returns 401 for unauthenticated)
  3. Checks user ownership (returns 404 if not found or not owned)
  4. For parent budgets: fetches all children and includes in deletion batch
  5. Deletes budget_rollovers for all affected budget IDs
  6. Deletes budget_amounts for all affected budget IDs
  7. Unlinks transactions (sets budget_id = null) — preserves transaction history
  8. Deletes child budgets first, then parent budget
  9. Returns detailed summary of what was deleted

  - Trash2 icon with "Verwijderen" label (data-testid="budget-delete-btn")
  - Confirmation dialog with warning about cascading effects
  - Shows different message for parent vs child budget deletion
  - Disabled state during deletion, error display
  - onDelete callback refreshes budgets and spending totals
- Spending totals automatically adjust because:
  - Spending is computed from transactions with non-null budget_id
  - After deletion, transactions have budget_id = null → excluded from spending totals
  - loadBudgets() and loadSpending() called after delete completes

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #164

### Feature #164: Completed next step removed from suggestions list - PASSING
- Fixed a bug where the `completedByDb` set in GET /api/next-steps was built from the
  next_step_completions table but never used for filtering steps
- Before fix: only real data state (hasTransactions, hasBudgets, etc.) marked steps as completed
- After fix: steps are completed if EITHER data state shows it OR the step was explicitly
  marked complete via POST /api/next-steps/complete (completedByDb.has(key))
- Updated NextStepSection client component to also load completed_steps from the API
  and filter them out (previously only loaded dismissed_steps)
- Flow verified:
  1. GET /api/next-steps returns pending_steps, completed_steps, dismissed_steps
  2. POST /api/next-steps/complete records {dismissed: false} in next_step_completions
  3. After completion, GET /api/next-steps excludes the step from pending_steps
  4. NextStepSection filters both dismissed AND completed steps from visible suggestions
- Database: next_step_completions table with unique(user_id, step_key) constraint
- Idempotent: upsert on conflict ensures completing same step twice doesn't create duplicates
- 9/9 verification checks passing at /api/verify-completed-next-step
- Zero console errors
- No mock data patterns found in production code

## Session 2026-02-17 - Feature #157

### Feature #157: Streak checkin twice on same day is idempotent - PASSING
- Existing streak checkin API at `app/api/streaks/checkin/route.ts` already has idempotency built in
- Key logic in `calculateStreak()` function (lines 54-105):
  - Gets ISO week for both the checkin date and the last_activity_date
  - If both are in the same ISO week → returns `action: "already_checked_in"` with unchanged counts
  - Same day is necessarily same ISO week, so same-day checkins are always idempotent
- Database path (`handleDbStreak`, lines 169-245):
  - When `action === 'already_checked_in'`, returns existing streak directly WITHOUT calling `.update()`
  - No database write occurs for duplicate checkins
- App_settings fallback path (`handleAppSettingsStreak`, lines 251-347):
  - When `action === 'already_checked_in'`, returns loginStreak directly WITHOUT calling `.upsert()`
  - No database write occurs for duplicate checkins
- 12/12 verification tests passing:
  1. POST /api/streaks/checkin endpoint exists ✅
  2. Code has same-week idempotency check ✅
  3. Same-week checkin returns unchanged current_count ✅
  4. Same-week checkin returns unchanged longest_count ✅
  5. Same-week checkin returns action "already_checked_in" ✅
  6. Database path skips UPDATE for already_checked_in ✅
  7. App_settings path skips update for already_checked_in ✅
  8. ISO week idempotency: same day is always same week ✅
  9. Checkin message for idempotent case is correct ✅
  10. No database write for idempotent checkin (DB path) ✅
  11. No database write for idempotent checkin (app_settings path) ✅
  12. Concurrent same-day checkins are safe ✅

## Session 2026-02-16 - Feature #140

### Feature #140: Dismissed next step persists across sessions - PASSING
- **Root cause**: NextStepSection component initialized dismissedKeys as empty Set() — never loaded previously dismissed steps from database
- **Fix**: Added useEffect to NextStepSection that fetches /api/next-steps on mount and initializes dismissedKeys from dismissed_steps array
- **Loading state**: Added `loaded` state flag to prevent flash of dismissed content before database query completes (renders null while loading)
- **Full persistence flow**:
  1. User clicks X on a next step suggestion → local state updated immediately + POST /api/next-steps/dismiss stores dismissal in next_step_completions table
  2. On component mount (page load, navigation, login) → useEffect fetches GET /api/next-steps → response includes dismissed_steps array → merges into local state
  3. Dismissed steps filtered out from visible suggestions
  4. Works across sessions because database is source of truth
- **Database**: next_step_completions table with UNIQUE(user_id, step_key) constraint, upsert behavior
- **API**: POST /api/next-steps/dismiss (upserts dismissed=true), GET /api/next-steps (returns dismissed_steps array)
- **Auth**: Dismiss API requires authentication (401 for unauthenticated), RLS enforces user isolation
- **Verification**: 8/8 tests passing via /api/verify-dismissed-next-step
- **Browser verification**: Dismiss workflow tested — dismissing all 3 Kern steps shows "Alles bij!" empty state
- **Console**: Zero errors on test pages
- **Mock data**: Zero mock data patterns found (grep clean)

## Session 2026-02-16 - Feature #139

### Feature #139: Back button after form submit does not re-submit - PASSING
- Added multi-layered back-button protection to the holdings form:
  1. **Idempotency key**: Each HoldingForm instance generates a crypto.randomUUID(), sent as X-Idempotency-Key header
  2. **Server-side cache**: Holdings API POST caches responses by idempotency key (5-min TTL), returns cached result on retry
  3. **Submitted state flag**: Form tracks `submitted` boolean to disable button and prevent handleSave from running again
  4. **History management**: HoldingsPage pushes history entry when modal opens; popstate listener closes modal on back button
  5. **FormSubmitted marker**: After successful save, history.replaceState marks formSubmitted=true; checked on mount to prevent re-open
- No HTML <form> element used - all submissions via fetch() onClick handler (no browser POST caching)
- 8/8 verification tests passing via /api/verify-back-button
- Zero console errors on test page
- No mock data patterns found in modified files

## Session 2026-02-16 - Feature #111

### Feature #111: Concurrent edit conflict handled for holdings - PASSING
- Implemented optimistic concurrency control for holdings PATCH endpoint
- Backend changes (app/api/holdings/route.ts):
  - PATCH endpoint now accepts optional `expected_updated_at` parameter
  - Before updating, reads current row's `updated_at` and compares to client's expected value
  - If timestamps differ (another session modified the row), returns 409 Conflict
  - 409 response includes `server_state` with current values for conflict resolution
  - Always bumps `updated_at` on write to enable future conflict checks
  - Without `expected_updated_at`, uses last-write-wins (backward compatible)
  - Assets fallback mode documented as last-write-wins only
- Frontend changes (app/(app)/core/assets/holdings/page.tsx):
  - HoldingEditForm sends `expected_updated_at: holding.updated_at` in PATCH requests
  - On 409 conflict: shows amber warning banner with "Bewerkingsconflict" heading
  - Shows server's current values (name, units, price) for comparison
  - Two resolution options:
    1. "Serverwaarden laden" — reloads form fields with server's current values
    2. "Toch overschrijven" — force-saves without expected_updated_at check
  - `data-testid="conflict-warning"`, `conflict-reload-btn`, `conflict-overwrite-btn`
- Verification endpoint: /api/verify-concurrent-edit (4/4 tests passing)
  - Tests: auth requirement, optimistic concurrency code, frontend handling, authenticated simulation
- Test page: /test-concurrent-edit — visual confirmation of all tests
- No mock data patterns found in modified files
- Zero console errors
- All changes committed in 3b9b82a (picked up by parallel agent commit)

### Feature #89: Streak system tracks consecutive weekly logins - PASSING

**Implementation:**
- Rewrote POST /api/streaks/checkin from daily to weekly streak logic
  - Uses ISO 8601 week numbers for consecutive week detection
  - getISOWeek() function correctly computes ISO week number
  - areConsecutiveWeeks() handles year boundary transitions (week 52/53 → week 1)

  - Gap of 2+ weeks resets current_count to 1 (longest_count preserved)

- After save, onSaved() callback triggers loadAssets() to refresh list from DB
- No localStorage/sessionStorage for asset data

### Feature #54: Debt creation and update persists - PASSING
- Debt creation: supabase.from('debts').insert(row) - direct PostgreSQL write
- Debt update (edit): supabase.from('debts').update(row).eq('id', debt.id)
- Debt balance update (revalue): supabase.from('debts').update({current_balance}).eq('id', entityId)
  - Also creates valuation history record via supabase.from('valuations').upsert()
- After any save, loadDebts() re-fetches full list from Supabase
- No localStorage/sessionStorage for debt data
- Page refresh triggers fresh loadDebts() via useEffect → shows updated balance
- Zero mock data patterns in codebase

## Session 2026-02-16 - Feature #130

### Feature #130: Goal progress bar reflects real current_value - PASSING
- Goal data loaded from Supabase `goals` table with `current_value` and `target_value` columns
- `computeGoalProgress(goal)` calculates percentage: `pct = Math.min(Math.round((current / target) * 100), 100)`
- Progress bar width set directly via `style={{ width: \`${progress.pct}%\` }}` in GoalSummaryRow component
- Will page (`app/(app)/will/page.tsx`) loads goals from Supabase, computes progress via `goals.map(g => computeGoalProgress(g))`
- PATCH /api/goals updates `current_value` in database and returns updated goal
- Linked assets/debts auto-update `current_value` from their real values
- Edge cases handled: 0 target returns 0%, over-target capped at 100%
- 8/8 API verification tests passing
- Visual verification: 30% bar for €3,000/€10,000 (✅ PASS), 50% bar for €5,000/€10,000 (✅ PASS)
- Zero console errors on clean page load
- No mock data patterns found

### Changes Made
- app/api/verify-goal-progress-bar/route.ts: Created 8-test verification endpoint
- app/test-goal-progress-bar/page.tsx: Created visual test page with progress bar demonstrations

## Session 2026-02-16 - Feature #138

### Feature #138: Multi-tab behavior does not corrupt data - PASSING
- Architecture verification confirms multi-tab safety by design:
  1. Dashboard (/dashboard) is SSR server component — fresh Supabase queries on every page visit, no client-side state caching
  2. Assets page (/core/assets) is client component with useEffect that loads data on mount — each tab gets independent React state
  3. Supabase browser client (lib/supabase/client.ts) uses createBrowserClient() which creates per-tab instances — no singleton pattern
  4. No global shared state patterns (globalThis, window.__) found in any production code (app/(app)/, lib/, components/)
  5. All CRUD operations write directly to Supabase PostgreSQL — no in-memory stores
  6. Asset creation inserts directly to 'assets' table via Supabase client
  7. Dashboard queries assets fresh on every render via Promise.all with 10 parallel queries
  8. Concurrent insert/read test passes — Supabase handles multi-client concurrency natively
- 8/8 API verification tests passing at /api/verify-multi-tab
- Visual verification via Playwright: test page shows 8/8 green checkmarks
- Zero console errors (only React DevTools info + HMR logs)
- Mock data grep checks: zero hits for globalThis, window.__, devStore, mockData, etc. in production code
- No shared mutable state between tabs — each tab has its own React state tree and Supabase client

### Changes Made
- app/api/verify-multi-tab/route.ts: Created 8-test verification endpoint
- app/test-multi-tab/page.tsx: Created visual test page with architecture explanation

- Pattern 4: positief_momentum - savings rate rising 3 months in a row
- Each alert has type, severity (info/warning), message, relatedModule
- Integrated into condense.ts as GEDETECTEERDE PATRONEN section
- Guards against false positives with insufficient history

## Session 2026-03-16 - Feature #81

### Feature #81: Fix DELETE transactie herberekening - PASSING
- Added replayTransactions() function to holding-transactions API route
  - Sorts all remaining transactions chronologically (date + created_at)
  - Buy: weighted average price calculation
  - Sell: units decrease, avg stays same; reset both to 0 if fully sold
  - Dividend: no effect on units/avg_purchase_price
- Updated DELETE handler to:
  1. Fetch transaction holding_id before deletion
  2. Delete the transaction from DB
  3. Fetch all remaining transactions for that holding
  4. Replay them to compute new units + avg_purchase_price
  5. Update the holding record with recalculated values
  6. Sync parent asset value via syncAssetValueFromHoldings()
- Both code paths covered: holding_transactions table + valuations fallback
- Added delete button with confirmation UI in holding-transaction-log.tsx
- Created holding-fav-widget.tsx (was missing, causing build error)

## Session 2026-03-17 - Feature #195

### Feature #195: BottomSheet swipe-to-dismiss vanuit content bij scroll-top - PASSING
- Added contentRef to scrollable content div for scrollTop detection
- Added handleContentTouchStart on content area (separate from drag handle)
- Gesture decision system: 'undecided' → 'scroll' or 'drag' with 5px threshold
- Content touch at scrollTop === 0 + swipe down → activates drag-dismiss
- Content touch with scroll position > 0 or swipe up → normal scroll
- Decision locked once made, preventing scroll/drag conflicts mid-gesture
- Existing drag handle behavior completely unchanged
- Zero TypeScript errors
